import 'atlas_object_track.dart';
import 'atlas_scan_observation.dart';

class AtlasObjectTrackerConfig {
  const AtlasObjectTrackerConfig({
    this.maxMatchDistance = 0.065,
    this.tentativeTtl = const Duration(milliseconds: 600),
    this.confirmedTtl = const Duration(milliseconds: 1400),
    this.cooldownDuration = const Duration(seconds: 4),
    this.confirmationMinHits = 3,
    this.centroidSmoothingAlpha = 0.30,
  });

  final double maxMatchDistance;
  final Duration tentativeTtl;
  final Duration confirmedTtl;
  final Duration cooldownDuration;
  final int confirmationMinHits;
  final double centroidSmoothingAlpha;
}

class AtlasObjectTracker {
  AtlasObjectTracker({this.config = const AtlasObjectTrackerConfig()}) {
    _validateConfig(config);
  }

  final AtlasObjectTrackerConfig config;
  final Map<String, AtlasObjectTrack> _tracks = <String, AtlasObjectTrack>{};
  int _nextTrackSequence = 1;

  List<AtlasObjectTrack> get tracks =>
      List<AtlasObjectTrack>.unmodifiable(_tracks.values);

  AtlasObjectTrack? trackForId(String trackId) => _tracks[trackId.trim()];

  List<AtlasObjectTrack> processFrame({
    required List<AtlasScanObservation> observations,
    required DateTime timestamp,
  }) {
    _removeStaleTracks(timestamp);

    final List<_MatchCandidate> candidates = <_MatchCandidate>[];

    for (
      int observationIndex = 0;
      observationIndex < observations.length;
      observationIndex++
    ) {
      final AtlasScanObservation observation = observations[observationIndex];
      final AtlasNormalizedPoint? observationCentroid = observation.centroid;
      if (observationCentroid == null) continue;

      for (final AtlasObjectTrack track in _tracks.values) {
        if (track.centroid == null) continue;

        final double distance = track.centroid!.distanceTo(observationCentroid);
        if (distance <= config.maxMatchDistance) {
          candidates.add(
            _MatchCandidate(
              observationIndex: observationIndex,
              trackId: track.trackId,
              distance: distance,
            ),
          );
        }
      }
    }

    candidates.sort(
      (_MatchCandidate a, _MatchCandidate b) =>
          a.distance.compareTo(b.distance),
    );

    final Set<int> claimedObservationIndexes = <int>{};
    final Set<String> claimedTrackIds = <String>{};

    for (final _MatchCandidate candidate in candidates) {
      if (claimedObservationIndexes.contains(candidate.observationIndex) ||
          claimedTrackIds.contains(candidate.trackId)) {
        continue;
      }

      final AtlasObjectTrack? existingTrack = _tracks[candidate.trackId];
      if (existingTrack == null) continue;

      final AtlasScanObservation observation =
          observations[candidate.observationIndex];

      AtlasObjectTrack updatedTrack = existingTrack.incorporateObservation(
        observation,
        centroidSmoothingAlpha: config.centroidSmoothingAlpha,
      );

      if (updatedTrack.isTentative &&
          updatedTrack.observationCount >= config.confirmationMinHits) {
        updatedTrack = updatedTrack.confirm();
      }

      _tracks[candidate.trackId] = updatedTrack;
      claimedObservationIndexes.add(candidate.observationIndex);
      claimedTrackIds.add(candidate.trackId);
    }

    for (
      int observationIndex = 0;
      observationIndex < observations.length;
      observationIndex++
    ) {
      if (claimedObservationIndexes.contains(observationIndex)) continue;

      final AtlasScanObservation observation = observations[observationIndex];
      final String trackId = _createTrackId(timestamp);

      AtlasObjectTrack newTrack = AtlasObjectTrack.createInitial(
        trackId: trackId,
        observation: observation,
      );

      if (config.confirmationMinHits <= 1) {
        newTrack = newTrack.confirm();
      }

      _tracks[trackId] = newTrack;
    }

    _removeStaleTracks(timestamp);
    return tracks;
  }

  bool markCooldown({required String trackId, required DateTime startedAt}) {
    final String normalizedTrackId = trackId.trim();
    final AtlasObjectTrack? track = _tracks[normalizedTrackId];
    if (track == null) return false;

    _tracks[normalizedTrackId] = track.startCooldown(startedAt);
    return true;
  }

  void reset() {
    _tracks.clear();
    _nextTrackSequence = 1;
  }

  void _removeStaleTracks(DateTime timestamp) {
    _tracks.removeWhere((String _, AtlasObjectTrack track) {
      if (track.isCooldown) {
        return timestamp.difference(track.lastSeenAt) > config.cooldownDuration;
      }

      final Duration elapsed = timestamp.difference(track.lastSeenAt);
      if (track.isTentative) {
        return elapsed > config.tentativeTtl;
      }

      return elapsed > config.confirmedTtl;
    });
  }

  String _createTrackId(DateTime timestamp) {
    return 'trk_${timestamp.microsecondsSinceEpoch}_${_nextTrackSequence++}';
  }

  static void _validateConfig(AtlasObjectTrackerConfig config) {
    if (!config.maxMatchDistance.isFinite ||
        config.maxMatchDistance <= 0.0 ||
        config.maxMatchDistance > 1.0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        config.maxMatchDistance,
        'maxMatchDistance',
        'maxMatchDistance must be greater than 0.0 and at most 1.0.',
      );
    }

    if (config.confirmationMinHits < 1) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        config.confirmationMinHits,
        'confirmationMinHits',
        'confirmationMinHits must be at least 1.',
      );
    }

    if (!config.centroidSmoothingAlpha.isFinite ||
        config.centroidSmoothingAlpha < 0.0 ||
        config.centroidSmoothingAlpha > 1.0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        config.centroidSmoothingAlpha,
        'centroidSmoothingAlpha',
        'centroidSmoothingAlpha must be between 0.0 and 1.0.',
      );
    }
  }
}

class _MatchCandidate {
  const _MatchCandidate({
    required this.observationIndex,
    required this.trackId,
    required this.distance,
  });

  final int observationIndex;
  final String trackId;
  final double distance;
}

import 'dart:async';

import '../../catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import '../../catalog/services/atlas_barcode_catalog_resolver.dart';
import '../engine/barcode_intelligence_engine.dart';
import '../engine/engine_context.dart';
import '../ledger/atlas_scan_ledger.dart';
import '../ledger/atlas_scan_ledger_entry.dart';
import '../models/scan_code.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import '../models/scanner_type.dart';
import '../pipeline/standard_scan_pipeline.dart';
import '../queue/atlas_resolution_job.dart';
import '../queue/atlas_resolution_queue.dart';
import '../results/atlas_continuous_scan_result.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_intent.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_performance_mode.dart';

typedef AtlasContinuousScanChanged = void Function();

class AtlasContinuousScanSnapshot {
  const AtlasContinuousScanSnapshot({
    required this.activeResolutionCount,
    required this.pendingResolutionCount,
    required this.observedCount,
    required this.queuedCount,
    required this.resolvingCount,
    required this.resolvedCount,
    required this.reviewCount,
    required this.failedCount,
    required this.committedCount,
  });

  final int activeResolutionCount;
  final int pendingResolutionCount;
  final int observedCount;
  final int queuedCount;
  final int resolvingCount;
  final int resolvedCount;
  final int reviewCount;
  final int failedCount;
  final int committedCount;

  int get completedCount => resolvedCount + reviewCount + committedCount;

  bool get hasWorkInFlight =>
      activeResolutionCount > 0 || pendingResolutionCount > 0;
}

/// Coordinates stable physical tracks with Atlas's existing resolution stack.
///
/// This class owns queueing and ledger state, but it does not mutate the Vault.
/// A resolved item remains separate from an explicitly committed item.
class AtlasContinuousScanCoordinator {
  AtlasContinuousScanCoordinator({
    AtlasResolutionQueue? resolutionQueue,
    AtlasScanLedger? ledger,
    AtlasBarcodeCatalogResolver? catalogResolver,
    this.onChanged,
  }) : resolutionQueue =
           resolutionQueue ?? AtlasResolutionQueue(maxConcurrentJobs: 4),
       ledger = ledger ?? AtlasScanLedger(),
       _catalogResolver = catalogResolver ?? AtlasBarcodeCatalogResolver();

  final AtlasResolutionQueue resolutionQueue;
  final AtlasScanLedger ledger;
  final AtlasBarcodeCatalogResolver _catalogResolver;
  final AtlasContinuousScanChanged? onChanged;

  final Map<String, AtlasContinuousScanResult> _resultsByTrack =
      <String, AtlasContinuousScanResult>{};

  bool _isDisposed = false;

  List<AtlasContinuousScanResult> get results {
    return List<AtlasContinuousScanResult>.unmodifiable(_resultsByTrack.values);
  }

  AtlasContinuousScanResult? resultForTrack(String trackId) {
    return _resultsByTrack[trackId.trim()];
  }

  AtlasContinuousScanSnapshot get snapshot {
    final AtlasResolutionQueueSnapshot queueSnapshot = resolutionQueue.snapshot;

    int observed = 0;
    int queued = 0;
    int resolving = 0;
    int resolved = 0;
    int review = 0;
    int failed = 0;
    int committed = 0;

    for (final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry in ledger.entries) {
      switch (entry.status) {
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed:
          observed++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued:
          queued++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving:
          resolving++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved:
          resolved++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview:
          review++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed:
          failed++;
        case AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed:
          committed++;
      }
    }

    return AtlasContinuousScanSnapshot(
      activeResolutionCount: queueSnapshot.activeCount,
      pendingResolutionCount: queueSnapshot.pendingCount,
      observedCount: observed,
      queuedCount: queued,
      resolvingCount: resolving,
      resolvedCount: resolved,
      reviewCount: review,
      failedCount: failed,
      committedCount: committed,
    );
  }

  /// Submits stable evidence from one physical track.
  ///
  /// Returns true only when a new background resolution job was accepted.
  bool submit({required String trackId, required ScanCode stableCode}) {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return false;
    }

    final String normalizedTrackId = trackId.trim();

    if (normalizedTrackId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(trackId, 'trackId', 'trackId cannot be empty.');
    }

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? existing = ledger.entryForTrack(
      normalizedTrackId,
    );

    if (existing != null) {
      return false;
    }

    ledger.observe(trackId: normalizedTrackId, stableCode: stableCode, at: now);

    ledger.markQueued(trackId: normalizedTrackId, at: now);

    _notifyChanged();

    _enqueueResolution(
      trackId: normalizedTrackId,
      stableCode: stableCode,
      createdAt: now,
    );

    return true;
  }

  /// Explicitly retries a failed physical track.
  ///
  /// Retry never creates a second ledger entry and never auto-commits. The
  /// original stable barcode evidence is reused for a new resolution attempt.
  bool retryFailedTrack({required String trackId}) {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return false;
    }

    final String normalizedTrackId = trackId.trim();

    if (normalizedTrackId.isEmpty ||
        resolutionQueue.containsTrack(normalizedTrackId)) {
      return false;
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entry = ledger.entryForTrack(normalizedTrackId);

    if (entry == null || !entry.hasFailed) {
      return false;
    }

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now().toUtc();

    ledger.markQueued(trackId: normalizedTrackId, at: now, allowRetry: true);

    _resultsByTrack.remove(normalizedTrackId);

    _notifyChanged();

    _enqueueResolution(
      trackId: normalizedTrackId,
      stableCode: entry.stableCode,
      createdAt: now,
    );

    return true;
  }

  bool markCommitted({required String trackId, DateTime? at}) {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return false;
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entry = ledger.entryForTrack(trackId);

    if (entry == null) {
      return false;
    }

    if (entry.isCommitted) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!entry.isResolved && !entry.needsReview) {
      return false;
    }

    ledger.markCommitted(trackId: trackId, at: at ?? DateTime.now().toUtc());

    _notifyChanged();
    return true;
  }

  bool applyInspection({
    required String trackId,
    required ScanSession session,
  }) {
    if (_isDisposed) return false;
    final normalizedTrackId = trackId.trim();
    final existing = _resultsByTrack[normalizedTrackId];
    if (existing == null || existing.session.sessionId != session.sessionId) {
      return false;
    }
    final assessment = session.authenticityContribution?.assessment;
    if (assessment == null) return false;
    _resultsByTrack[normalizedTrackId] = existing.copyWith(session: session);
    ledger.applyInspectionReview(
      trackId: normalizedTrackId,
      requiresReview:
          assessment.requiresReview ||
          session.requiresUserAction ||
          !existing.catalogResolution.hasSingleMatch,
      at: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
    );
    _notifyChanged();
    return true;
  }

  bool get canResetCompletedSession {
    final List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry> entries = ledger.entries;

    return !_isDisposed &&
        resolutionQueue.snapshot.isIdle &&
        entries.isNotEmpty &&
        entries.every((AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry) => entry.isCommitted);
  }

  bool resetCompletedSession() {
    if (!canResetCompletedSession) {
      return false;
    }

    ledger.clear();
    _resultsByTrack.clear();
    _notifyChanged();
    return true;
  }

  void dispose() {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    _isDisposed = true;
    resolutionQueue.dispose();
  }

  void _enqueueResolution({
    required String trackId,
    required ScanCode stableCode,
    required DateTime createdAt,
  }) {
    final String jobId = _createJobId(trackId: trackId, createdAt: createdAt);

    unawaited(
      resolutionQueue
          .enqueue(
            AtlasResolutionJob(
              jobId: jobId,
              trackId: trackId,
              createdAt: createdAt,
              execute: () =>
                  _resolveTrack(trackId: trackId, stableCode: stableCode),
            ),
          )
          .catchError((Object error, StackTrace stackTrace) {
            _handleQueueFailure(trackId: trackId, error: error);
          }),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _resolveTrack({
    required String trackId,
    required ScanCode stableCode,
  }) async {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    final DateTime startedAt = DateTime.now().toUtc();

    ledger.markResolving(trackId: trackId, at: startedAt);
    _notifyChanged();

    try {
      final String executionId = _createExecutionId(
        startedAt,
        stableCode.normalizedValue,
      );

      final EngineContext engineContext = EngineContext(
        requestId: executionId,
        createdAt: startedAt,
        connectivity: EngineConnectivity.unknown,
        deviceType: EngineDeviceType.mobile,
        deviceCapabilities: const <String>{
          BarcodeIntelligenceEngine.capabilityId,
        },
        attributes: <String, Object?>{
          'scanSource': ScanCodeSource.camera.name,
          'scannerType': ScannerType.mobileScanner.name,
          'barcodeFormat': stableCode.format.name,
          'trackId': trackId,
          'continuousScan': true,
        },
      );

      final ScanSession initialSession = ScanSession(
        sessionId: executionId,
        startedAt: startedAt,
        status: ScanSessionStatus.analyzing,
        intent: ScanIntent.identifyProduct,
        performanceMode: ScanPerformanceMode.fastBarcodeOnly,
        scannerType: ScannerType.mobileScanner,
        detectedCodes: <ScanCode>[stableCode],
        bestCandidate: stableCode,
      );

      final pipeline = StandardScanPipeline.create(engineContext);
      final result = await pipeline.process(initialSession, engineContext);

      final ScanSession finalSession = result.finalSession;

      final ScanCode? resolvedCode =
          finalSession.bestCandidate ?? _firstDetectedCode(finalSession);

      final AtlasBarcodeResolution catalogResolution;

      if (resolvedCode == null ||
          !resolvedCode.isValid ||
          !resolvedCode.isProductBarcode) {
        catalogResolution = AtlasBarcodeResolution.invalid(
          barcode: resolvedCode?.normalizedValue ?? '',
        );
      } else if (finalSession.catalogProduct != null) {
        catalogResolution = AtlasBarcodeResolution.matches(
          barcode: resolvedCode.normalizedValue,
          matches: <AtlasCatalogProduct>[finalSession.catalogProduct!],
        );
      } else {
        catalogResolution = await _catalogResolver.resolve(
          resolvedCode.normalizedValue,
        );
      }

      final DateTime completedAt = DateTime.now().toUtc();

      final AtlasContinuousScanResult continuousResult =
          AtlasContinuousScanResult(
            trackId: trackId,
            session: finalSession,
            catalogResolution: catalogResolution,
            completedAt: completedAt,
          );

      _resultsByTrack[trackId] = continuousResult;

      if (continuousResult.requiresUserReview) {
        ledger.markNeedsReview(
          trackId: trackId,
          resolutionSessionId: finalSession.sessionId,
          at: completedAt,
        );
      } else {
        ledger.markResolved(
          trackId: trackId,
          resolutionSessionId: finalSession.sessionId,
          at: completedAt,
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      ledger.markFailed(
        trackId: trackId,
        error: error,
        at: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
      );
      rethrow;
    } finally {
      _notifyChanged();
    }
  }

  void _handleQueueFailure({required String trackId, required Object error}) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entry = ledger.entryForTrack(trackId);

    if (entry == null || entry.hasFailed) {
      _notifyChanged();
      return;
    }

    if (entry.isQueued) {
      ledger.markResolving(trackId: trackId, at: DateTime.now().toUtc());
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? current = ledger.entryForTrack(trackId);

    if (current != null && current.isResolving) {
      ledger.markFailed(
        trackId: trackId,
        error: error,
        at: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
      );
    }

    _notifyChanged();
  }

  ScanCode? _firstDetectedCode(ScanSession session) {
    if (session.detectedCodes.isEmpty) {
      return null;
    }

    return session.detectedCodes.first;
  }

  String _createExecutionId(DateTime createdAt, String code) {
    final int codeHash = code.hashCode.abs();

    return 'scan_'
        '${createdAt.microsecondsSinceEpoch}_'
        '$codeHash';
  }

  String _createJobId({required String trackId, required DateTime createdAt}) {
    return 'resolution_'
        '${createdAt.microsecondsSinceEpoch}_'
        '${trackId.hashCode.abs()}';
  }

  void _notifyChanged() {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    onChanged?.call();
  }
}

import '../../catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import '../../catalog/services/atlas_barcode_catalog_resolver.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';

/// Immutable completed background-resolution result for one physical track.
class AtlasContinuousScanResult {
  const AtlasContinuousScanResult({
    required this.trackId,
    required this.session,
    required this.catalogResolution,
    required this.completedAt,
  });

  final String trackId;
  final ScanSession session;
  final AtlasBarcodeResolution catalogResolution;
  final DateTime completedAt;

  bool get isOfflineResult => session.isOffline;

  bool get requiresSync => session.requiresSync;

  bool get requiresUserReview {
    return session.requiresUserAction || !catalogResolution.hasSingleMatch;
  }

  AtlasCatalogProduct? get resolvedCatalogProduct {
    return session.catalogProduct ?? catalogResolution.singleMatch;
  }

  AtlasContinuousScanResult copyWith({ScanSession? session}) {
    return AtlasContinuousScanResult(
      trackId: trackId,
      session: session ?? this.session,
      catalogResolution: catalogResolution,
      completedAt: completedAt,
    );
  }
}

import '../models/scan_code.dart';
import 'atlas_scan_ledger_entry.dart';

/// In-memory ledger for one continuous scanner workflow.
///
/// Entries are keyed by physical track ID rather than barcode or product ID so
/// two identical collectibles with the same UPC can remain separate items.
class AtlasScanLedger {
  final Map<String, AtlasScanLedgerEntry> _entries =
      <String, AtlasScanLedgerEntry>{};

  List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry> get entries {
    return List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry>.unmodifiable(_entries.values);
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entryForTrack(String trackId) {
    return _entries[trackId.trim()];
  }

  bool containsTrack(String trackId) {
    return _entries.containsKey(trackId.trim());
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry observe({
    required String trackId,
    required ScanCode stableCode,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final String normalizedTrackId = _normalizeTrackId(trackId);
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? existing = _entries[normalizedTrackId];

    if (existing != null) {
      return existing;
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = AtlasScanLedgerEntry(
      trackId: normalizedTrackId,
      stableCode: stableCode,
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed,
      createdAt: at,
      updatedAt: at,
      attemptCount: 0,
    );

    _entries[normalizedTrackId] = entry;

    return entry;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markQueued({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
    bool allowRetry = false,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (entry.isCommitted) {
      throw StateError(
        'Committed track "${entry.trackId}" cannot be queued again.',
      );
    }

    if (entry.hasFailed && !allowRetry) {
      throw StateError(
        'Failed track "${entry.trackId}" requires an explicit retry.',
      );
    }

    if (entry.isQueued || entry.isResolving) {
      return entry;
    }

    if (entry.isResolved || entry.needsReview) {
      throw StateError(
        'Resolved track "${entry.trackId}" cannot be queued again.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued,
      updatedAt: at,
      clearFailureMessage: allowRetry,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markResolving({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isQueued) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be queued before resolving.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving,
      updatedAt: at,
      attemptCount: entry.attemptCount + 1,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markResolved({
    required String trackId,
    required String resolutionSessionId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before it can resolve.',
      );
    }

    final String normalizedSessionId = resolutionSessionId.trim();

    if (normalizedSessionId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        resolutionSessionId,
        'resolutionSessionId',
        'resolutionSessionId cannot be empty.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved,
      updatedAt: at,
      resolutionSessionId: normalizedSessionId,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markNeedsReview({
    required String trackId,
    required String resolutionSessionId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before review.',
      );
    }

    final String normalizedSessionId = resolutionSessionId.trim();

    if (normalizedSessionId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        resolutionSessionId,
        'resolutionSessionId',
        'resolutionSessionId cannot be empty.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview,
      updatedAt: at,
      resolutionSessionId: normalizedSessionId,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markFailed({
    required String trackId,
    required Object error,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before it can fail.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed,
      updatedAt: at,
      failureMessage: error.toString(),
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markCommitted({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (entry.isCommitted) {
      return entry;
    }

    if (!entry.isResolved && !entry.needsReview) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolved before commit.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed,
      updatedAt: at,
      committedAt: at,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry applyInspectionReview({
    required String trackId,
    required bool requiresReview,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final entry = _requireEntry(trackId);
    if (entry.isCommitted || (!entry.isResolved && !entry.needsReview)) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" is not available for inspection review.',
      );
    }
    final updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: requiresReview
          ? AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview
          : AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved,
      updatedAt: at,
    );
    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;
    return updated;
  }

  void clear() {
    _entries.clear();
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry _requireEntry(String trackId) {
    final String normalizedTrackId = _normalizeTrackId(trackId);
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entry = _entries[normalizedTrackId];

    if (entry == null) {
      throw StateError(
        'No scan ledger entry exists for track "$normalizedTrackId".',
      );
    }

    return entry;
  }

  String _normalizeTrackId(String trackId) {
    final String normalized = trackId.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(trackId, 'trackId', 'trackId cannot be empty.');
    }

    return normalized;
  }
}

import '../models/scan_code.dart';

enum AtlasScanLedgerStatus {
  observed,
  queued,
  resolving,
  resolved,
  needsReview,
  failed,
  committed,
}

/// Immutable lifecycle record for one physical scanner track.
///
/// A resolved track is deliberately not the same thing as a committed track.
/// Collection quantity changes must only occur after an explicit commit.
class AtlasScanLedgerEntry {
  const AtlasScanLedgerEntry({
    required this.trackId,
    required this.stableCode,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.attemptCount,
    this.resolutionSessionId,
    this.failureMessage,
    this.committedAt,
  });

  final String trackId;
  final ScanCode stableCode;
  final AtlasScanLedgerStatus status;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final int attemptCount;
  final String? resolutionSessionId;
  final String? failureMessage;
  final DateTime? committedAt;

  bool get isObserved => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed;
  bool get isQueued => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued;
  bool get isResolving => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving;
  bool get isResolved => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved;
  bool get needsReview => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview;
  bool get hasFailed => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed;
  bool get isCommitted => status == AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed;

  bool get canRetry => hasFailed;

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry copyWith({
    AtlasScanLedgerStatus? status,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
    int? attemptCount,
    String? resolutionSessionId,
    String? failureMessage,
    DateTime? committedAt,
    bool clearFailureMessage = false,
  }) {
    return AtlasScanLedgerEntry(
      trackId: trackId,
      stableCode: stableCode,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      createdAt: createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      attemptCount: attemptCount ?? this.attemptCount,
      resolutionSessionId: resolutionSessionId ?? this.resolutionSessionId,
      failureMessage: clearFailureMessage
          ? null
          : failureMessage ?? this.failureMessage,
      committedAt: committedAt ?? this.committedAt,
    );
  }
}

FILE: .\lib\atlas\scan\queue\atlas_resolution_queue.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:collection';

import 'atlas_resolution_job.dart';

/// Snapshot of queue activity for diagnostics and future scanner HUDs.
class AtlasResolutionQueueSnapshot {
  const AtlasResolutionQueueSnapshot({
    required this.activeCount,
    required this.pendingCount,
    required this.activeTrackIds,
    required this.pendingTrackIds,
  });

  final int activeCount;
  final int pendingCount;
  final Set<String> activeTrackIds;
  final Set<String> pendingTrackIds;

  bool get isIdle => activeCount == 0 && pendingCount == 0;
}

class AtlasResolutionQueue {
  AtlasResolutionQueue({this.maxConcurrentJobs = 4}) {
    if (maxConcurrentJobs < 1) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        maxConcurrentJobs,
        'maxConcurrentJobs',
        'maxConcurrentJobs must be at least 1.',
      );
    }
  }

  final int maxConcurrentJobs;

  final Queue<_QueuedResolutionJob> _pending = Queue<_QueuedResolutionJob>();

  final Set<String> _activeTrackIds = <String>{};
  final Set<String> _pendingTrackIds = <String>{};

  int _activeCount = 0;
  bool _isDisposed = false;

  AtlasResolutionQueueSnapshot get snapshot {
    return AtlasResolutionQueueSnapshot(
      activeCount: _activeCount,
      pendingCount: _pending.length,
      activeTrackIds: Set<String>.unmodifiable(_activeTrackIds),
      pendingTrackIds: Set<String>.unmodifiable(_pendingTrackIds),
    );
  }

  bool containsTrack(String trackId) {
    final String normalized = trackId.trim();

    return _activeTrackIds.contains(normalized) ||
        _pendingTrackIds.contains(normalized);
  }

  Future<void> enqueue(AtlasResolutionJob job) {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return Future<void>.error(
        StateError('AtlasResolutionQueue has been disposed.'),
      );
    }

    final String trackId = job.trackId.trim();

    if (containsTrack(trackId)) {
      return Future<void>.error(
        StateError(
          'A resolution job for track "$trackId" is already queued or active.',
        ),
      );
    }

    final Completer<void> completer = Completer<void>();

    _pending.add(_QueuedResolutionJob(job: job, completer: completer));
    _pendingTrackIds.add(trackId);

    _drain();

    return completer.future;
  }

  void dispose() {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    _isDisposed = true;

    while (_pending.isNotEmpty) {
      final _QueuedResolutionJob queued = _pending.removeFirst();
      _pendingTrackIds.remove(queued.job.trackId.trim());

      if (!queued.completer.isCompleted) {
        queued.completer.completeError(
          StateError('Resolution queue disposed before the job started.'),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _drain() {
    if (_isDisposed) {
      return;
    }

    while (_activeCount < maxConcurrentJobs && _pending.isNotEmpty) {
      final _QueuedResolutionJob queued = _pending.removeFirst();
      final String trackId = queued.job.trackId.trim();

      _pendingTrackIds.remove(trackId);
      _activeTrackIds.add(trackId);
      _activeCount++;

      unawaited(_run(queued));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _run(_QueuedResolutionJob queued) async {
    final String trackId = queued.job.trackId.trim();

    try {
      await queued.job.execute();

      if (!queued.completer.isCompleted) {
        queued.completer.complete();
      }
    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
      if (!queued.completer.isCompleted) {
        queued.completer.completeError(error, stackTrace);
      }
    } finally {
      _activeTrackIds.remove(trackId);
      _activeCount--;

      if (!_isDisposed) {
        _drain();
      }
    }
  }
}

class _QueuedResolutionJob {
  const _QueuedResolutionJob({required this.job, required this.completer});

  final AtlasResolutionJob job;
  final Completer<void> completer;
}

import '../../catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import '../contributions/contribution_collection.dart';
import '../contributions/contribution_conflict_policy.dart';
import '../contributions/intelligence_contribution.dart';
import '../contributions/authenticity_contribution.dart';
import '../contributions/visual_inspection_contribution.dart';
import '../../identity/product/atlas_product_id.dart';
import '../projection/scan_session_projector.dart';
import '../resolution/resolved_product_snapshot.dart';
import '../results/collector_item_status.dart';
import '../results/market_value_snapshot.dart';
import '../results/scan_variant_status.dart';
import '../workflow/identity_resolution_status.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_intent.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_performance_mode.dart';
import 'scan_code.dart';
import 'scan_image.dart';
import 'scanner_type.dart';
import 'visual_identity.dart';

// Describes the lifecycle of an Atlas scan session.
enum ScanSessionStatus {
  created,
  scanning,
  analyzing,
  resolving,
  awaitingConfirmation,
  completed,
  cancelled,
  failed,
}

// Immutable orchestration model for one Atlas scanning workflow.
//
// A session begins when a collector opens the scanner and continues
// through code detection, visual analysis, identity resolution,
// market lookup, Vault matching, and briefing generation.
class ScanSession {
  ScanSession({
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.intent,
    required this.performanceMode,
    required this.scannerType,
    this.status = ScanSessionStatus.created,
    this.completedAt,
    List<ScanCode> detectedCodes = const [],
    this.bestCandidate,
    this.visualIdentity = const VisualIdentity(),
    this.resolutionStatus = IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted,
    this.atlasProductId,
    this.resolvedProduct,
    this.marketSnapshot,
    this.collectorStatus,
    this.variantResolution,
    ContributionCollection? contributions,
    List<ScanImage> images = const [],
    this.confidence = 0.0,
    this.isOffline = false,
    this.requiresSync = false,
    List<String> notes = const [],
    List<String> warnings = const [],
  }) : detectedCodes = List<ScanCode>.unmodifiable(detectedCodes),
       contributions = contributions ?? ContributionCollection.empty,
       images = List<ScanImage>.unmodifiable(images),
       notes = List<String>.unmodifiable(notes),
       warnings = List<String>.unmodifiable(warnings);

  final String sessionId;

  final DateTime startedAt;

  final DateTime? completedAt;

  final ScanSessionStatus status;

  final ScanIntent intent;

  final ScanPerformanceMode performanceMode;

  final ScannerType scannerType;

  final List<ScanCode> detectedCodes;

  final ScanCode? bestCandidate;

  final VisualIdentity visualIdentity;

  final IdentityResolutionStatus resolutionStatus;

  final AtlasProductId? atlasProductId;

  final ResolvedProductSnapshot? resolvedProduct;

  final MarketValueSnapshot? marketSnapshot;

  final CollectorItemStatus? collectorStatus;

  final ScanVariantResolution? variantResolution;

  final ContributionCollection contributions;

  final List<ScanImage> images;

  final double confidence;

  final bool isOffline;

  final bool requiresSync;

  final List<String> notes;

  final List<String> warnings;

  bool get hasDetectedCodes {
    return detectedCodes.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasBestCandidate {
    return bestCandidate != null;
  }

  bool get hasVisualEvidence {
    return visualIdentity.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasResolvedProduct {
    return atlasProductId != null && resolutionStatus.isResolved;
  }

  bool get hasMarketData {
    return marketSnapshot?.hasMarketEvidence ?? false;
  }

  bool get hasCollectorStatus {
    return collectorStatus != null;
  }

  bool get hasVariantResolution {
    return variantResolution != null;
  }

  bool get hasContributions {
    return contributions.isNotEmpty;
  }

  VisualInspectionContribution? get visualInspectionContribution =>
      latestContributionOfType<VisualInspectionContribution>();

  AuthenticityContribution? get authenticityContribution =>
      latestContributionOfType<AuthenticityContribution>();

  bool get hasAuthenticityAssessment => authenticityContribution != null;

  int get contributionCount {
    return contributions.length;
  }

  bool hasContributionOfType<T extends IntelligenceContribution>() {
    return contributions.ofType<T>().isNotEmpty;
  }

  T? latestContributionOfType<T extends IntelligenceContribution>() {
    return contributions.latestOfType<T>();
  }

  bool get requiresVariantConfirmation {
    return variantResolution?.requiresConfirmation ?? false;
  }

  bool get hasImages {
    return images.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasOriginalImage {
    return images.any((ScanImage image) => image.isOriginal);
  }

  bool get hasProcessedImage {
    return images.any((ScanImage image) => image.isProcessed);
  }

  bool get isActive {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.scanning ||
        status == ScanSessionStatus.analyzing ||
        status == ScanSessionStatus.resolving;
  }

  bool get isCompleted {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.completed;
  }

  AtlasCatalogProduct? get catalogProduct {
    return resolvedProduct?.toCatalogProduct();
  }

  bool get requiresUserAction {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.awaitingConfirmation ||
        resolutionStatus.requiresUserReview ||
        requiresVariantConfirmation;
  }

  bool get canBuildBriefing {
    return hasResolvedProduct && resolutionStatus.isResolved;
  }

  Duration get elapsed {
    final DateTime end = completedAt ?? DateTime.now();

    return end.difference(startedAt);
  }

  ScanImage? get originalImage {
    for (final ScanImage image in images) {
      if (image.type == ScanImageType.original) {
        return image;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  ScanImage? get processedImage {
    for (final ScanImage image in images) {
      if (image.type == ScanImageType.processed) {
        return image;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  ScanSession copyWith({
    String? sessionId,
    DateTime? startedAt,
    DateTime? completedAt,
    ScanSessionStatus? status,
    ScanIntent? intent,
    ScanPerformanceMode? performanceMode,
    ScannerType? scannerType,
    List<ScanCode>? detectedCodes,
    ScanCode? bestCandidate,
    VisualIdentity? visualIdentity,
    IdentityResolutionStatus? resolutionStatus,
    AtlasProductId? atlasProductId,
    ResolvedProductSnapshot? resolvedProduct,
    MarketValueSnapshot? marketSnapshot,
    CollectorItemStatus? collectorStatus,
    ScanVariantResolution? variantResolution,
    ContributionCollection? contributions,
    List<ScanImage>? images,
    double? confidence,
    bool? isOffline,
    bool? requiresSync,
    List<String>? notes,
    List<String>? warnings,
  }) {
    return ScanSession(
      sessionId: sessionId ?? this.sessionId,
      startedAt: startedAt ?? this.startedAt,
      completedAt: completedAt ?? this.completedAt,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      intent: intent ?? this.intent,
      performanceMode: performanceMode ?? this.performanceMode,
      scannerType: scannerType ?? this.scannerType,
      detectedCodes: detectedCodes ?? this.detectedCodes,
      bestCandidate: bestCandidate ?? this.bestCandidate,
      visualIdentity: visualIdentity ?? this.visualIdentity,
      resolutionStatus: resolutionStatus ?? this.resolutionStatus,
      atlasProductId: atlasProductId ?? this.atlasProductId,
      resolvedProduct: resolvedProduct ?? this.resolvedProduct,
      marketSnapshot: marketSnapshot ?? this.marketSnapshot,
      collectorStatus: collectorStatus ?? this.collectorStatus,
      variantResolution: variantResolution ?? this.variantResolution,
      contributions: contributions ?? this.contributions,
      images: images ?? this.images,
      confidence: confidence ?? this.confidence,
      isOffline: isOffline ?? this.isOffline,
      requiresSync: requiresSync ?? this.requiresSync,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      warnings: warnings ?? this.warnings,
    );
  }

  ScanSession addDetectedCode(ScanCode code, {bool makeBestCandidate = false}) {
    final bool alreadyDetected = detectedCodes.any((ScanCode existing) {
      return existing.format == code.format &&
          existing.normalizedValue == code.normalizedValue;
    });

    final List<ScanCode> updatedCodes = alreadyDetected
        ? detectedCodes
        : <ScanCode>[...detectedCodes, code];

    return copyWith(
      detectedCodes: updatedCodes,
      bestCandidate: makeBestCandidate ? code : bestCandidate,
    );
  }

  ScanSession addContribution(
    IntelligenceContribution contribution, {
    ContributionConflictPolicy conflictPolicy =
        ContributionConflictPolicy.reject,
  }) {
    final ContributionCollection updatedContributions = contributions.add(
      contribution,
      conflictPolicy: conflictPolicy,
    );

    return _withProjectedContributions(updatedContributions);
  }

  ScanSession addContributions(
    Iterable<IntelligenceContribution> values, {
    ContributionConflictPolicy conflictPolicy =
        ContributionConflictPolicy.reject,
  }) {
    final ContributionCollection updatedContributions = contributions.addAll(
      values,
      conflictPolicy: conflictPolicy,
    );

    return _withProjectedContributions(updatedContributions);
  }

  ScanSession _withProjectedContributions(
    ContributionCollection updatedContributions,
  ) {
    final ScanSessionProjection projection = ScanSessionProjector.project(
      contributions: updatedContributions,
      currentStatus: status,
      currentResolutionStatus: resolutionStatus,
      currentAtlasProductId: atlasProductId,
      currentResolvedProduct: resolvedProduct,
      currentConfidence: confidence,
      isOffline: isOffline,
    );

    return copyWith(
      contributions: updatedContributions,
      status: projection.status,
      resolutionStatus: projection.resolutionStatus,
      atlasProductId: projection.atlasProductId,
      resolvedProduct: projection.resolvedProduct,
      confidence: projection.confidence,
    );
  }

  ScanSession removeContributionById(String contributionId) {
    final ContributionCollection updated = contributions.removeById(
      contributionId,
    );

    if (identical(updated, contributions)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(contributions: updated);
  }

  ScanSession removeContributionsFromEngine(String engineId) {
    final ContributionCollection updated = contributions.removeFromEngine(
      engineId,
    );

    if (identical(updated, contributions)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(contributions: updated);
  }

  ScanSession addImage(ScanImage image) {
    final List<ScanImage> updatedImages =
        images
            .where((ScanImage existing) => existing.imageId != image.imageId)
            .toList()
          ..add(image);

    return copyWith(images: updatedImages);
  }

  ScanSession addNote(String note) {
    final String normalized = note.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty || notes.contains(normalized)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(notes: <String>[...notes, normalized]);
  }

  ScanSession addWarning(String warning) {
    final String normalized = warning.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty || warnings.contains(normalized)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(warnings: <String>[...warnings, normalized]);
  }

  @override
  String toString() {
    return 'ScanSession('
        'sessionId: $sessionId, '
        'status: ${status.name}, '
        'intent: ${intent.name}, '
        'codes: ${detectedCodes.length}, '
        'images: ${images.length}, '
        'resolutionStatus: ${resolutionStatus.name}, '
        'atlasProductId: $atlasProductId, '
        'confidence: $confidence, '
        'isOffline: $isOffline, '
        'requiresSync: $requiresSync'
        ')';
  }
}

// Describes the role an image plays within an Atlas scan session.
enum ScanImageType {
  original,
  processed,
  thumbnail,
  frontBox,
  backBox,
  bottomBox,
  barcode,
  sticker,
  autograph,
  certification,
  condition,
  unknown,
}

// Describes the current processing state of a scan image.
enum ScanImageStatus {
  captured,
  queued,
  uploading,
  uploaded,
  processing,
  processed,
  approved,
  rejected,
  failed,
}

// Immutable image reference associated with a scan session.
//
// Images may initially exist only on the local device. Remote storage
// information can be added later after upload or synchronization.
class ScanImage {
  const ScanImage({
    required this.imageId,
    required this.type,
    required this.status,
    required this.capturedAt,
    this.localPath,
    this.remoteUrl,
    this.mimeType,
    this.width,
    this.height,
    this.byteSize,
    this.imageFingerprint,
    this.processingNotes = const [],
  });

  final String imageId;

  final ScanImageType type;

  final ScanImageStatus status;

  final DateTime capturedAt;

  final String? localPath;

  final String? remoteUrl;

  final String? mimeType;

  final int? width;

  final int? height;

  final int? byteSize;

  final String? imageFingerprint;

  final List<String> processingNotes;

  bool get hasLocalPath {
    return localPath != null && localPath!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasRemoteUrl {
    return remoteUrl != null && remoteUrl!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasFingerprint {
    return imageFingerprint != null && imageFingerprint!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get isAvailable {
    return hasLocalPath || hasRemoteUrl;
  }

  bool get isOriginal {
    return type == ScanImageType.original;
  }

  bool get isProcessed {
    return status == ScanImageStatus.processed ||
        status == ScanImageStatus.approved;
  }

  bool get requiresSync {
    return hasLocalPath && !hasRemoteUrl;
  }

  ScanImage copyWith({
    String? imageId,
    ScanImageType? type,
    ScanImageStatus? status,
    DateTime? capturedAt,
    String? localPath,
    String? remoteUrl,
    String? mimeType,
    int? width,
    int? height,
    int? byteSize,
    String? imageFingerprint,
    List<String>? processingNotes,
  }) {
    return ScanImage(
      imageId: imageId ?? this.imageId,
      type: type ?? this.type,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      capturedAt: capturedAt ?? this.capturedAt,
      localPath: localPath ?? this.localPath,
      remoteUrl: remoteUrl ?? this.remoteUrl,
      mimeType: mimeType ?? this.mimeType,
      width: width ?? this.width,
      height: height ?? this.height,
      byteSize: byteSize ?? this.byteSize,
      imageFingerprint: imageFingerprint ?? this.imageFingerprint,
      processingNotes: processingNotes ?? this.processingNotes,
    );
  }
}

// Visual evidence collected while identifying a collectible.
//
// This information supplements traditional identifiers such as UPC,
// QR, and EAN. Over time Atlas Intelligence will continue enriching
// this model as new computer vision capabilities are introduced.
class VisualIdentity {
  const VisualIdentity({
    this.boxNumber,
    this.detectedStickers = const [],
    this.detectedLogos = const [],
    this.detectedAutographs = const [],
    this.detectedCharacters = const [],
    this.imageFingerprint,
    this.visualEmbedding,
    this.colorSignature,
    this.boxConditionScore,
    this.windowConditionScore,
    this.confidence = 0.0,
  });

  final String? boxNumber;

  final List<String> detectedStickers;

  final List<String> detectedLogos;

  final List<String> detectedAutographs;

  final List<String> detectedCharacters;

  final String? imageFingerprint;

  // Reserved for future AI vector embeddings.
  final String? visualEmbedding;

  // Reserved for future color histogram matching.
  final String? colorSignature;

  final double? boxConditionScore;

  final double? windowConditionScore;

  final double confidence;

  bool get hasBoxNumber => boxNumber != null && boxNumber!.isNotEmpty;

  bool get hasStickerEvidence => detectedStickers.isNotEmpty;

  bool get hasAutographEvidence => detectedAutographs.isNotEmpty;

  bool get hasCharacterEvidence => detectedCharacters.isNotEmpty;

  bool get hasImageFingerprint =>
      imageFingerprint != null &&
          imageFingerprint!.isNotEmpty;

  bool get hasVisualEmbedding =>
      visualEmbedding != null &&
          visualEmbedding!.isNotEmpty;

  bool get isEmpty {
    return !hasBoxNumber &&
        !hasStickerEvidence &&
        !hasAutographEvidence &&
        !hasCharacterEvidence &&
        !hasImageFingerprint &&
        !hasVisualEmbedding;
  }

  bool get isNotEmpty => !isEmpty;

  VisualIdentity copyWith({
    String? boxNumber,
    List<String>? detectedStickers,
    List<String>? detectedLogos,
    List<String>? detectedAutographs,
    List<String>? detectedCharacters,
    String? imageFingerprint,
    String? visualEmbedding,
    String? colorSignature,
    double? boxConditionScore,
    double? windowConditionScore,
    double? confidence,
  }) {
    return VisualIdentity(
      boxNumber: boxNumber ?? this.boxNumber,
      detectedStickers:
      detectedStickers ?? this.detectedStickers,
      detectedLogos:
      detectedLogos ?? this.detectedLogos,
      detectedAutographs:
      detectedAutographs ??
          this.detectedAutographs,
      detectedCharacters:
      detectedCharacters ??
          this.detectedCharacters,
      imageFingerprint:
      imageFingerprint ?? this.imageFingerprint,
      visualEmbedding:
      visualEmbedding ?? this.visualEmbedding,
      colorSignature:
      colorSignature ?? this.colorSignature,
      boxConditionScore:
      boxConditionScore ??
          this.boxConditionScore,
      windowConditionScore:
      windowConditionScore ??
          this.windowConditionScore,
      confidence:
      confidence ?? this.confidence,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:google_mlkit_text_recognition/google_mlkit_text_recognition.dart';

abstract interface class AtlasTextRecognition {
  Future<String> recognize(String path);
  Future<void> close();
}

final class AtlasTextRecognitionService implements AtlasTextRecognition {
  AtlasTextRecognitionService({TextRecognizer? recognizer})
    : _recognizer = recognizer ?? TextRecognizer();

  final TextRecognizer _recognizer;

  @override
  Future<String> recognize(String path) async {
    final normalized = path.trim();
    if (normalized.isEmpty) return '';
    final result = await _recognizer.processImage(
      InputImage.fromFilePath(normalized),
    );
    return result.text.trim();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> close() => _recognizer.close();
}

import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import '../results/collector_item_status.dart';
import '../results/market_value_snapshot.dart';
import '../results/scan_variant_status.dart';
import '../workflow/identity_resolution_status.dart';
import 'atlas_scan_briefing.dart';

class AtlasScanBriefingEngine {
  const AtlasScanBriefingEngine._();

  static AtlasScanBriefing build(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    final List<String> highlights = <String>[];

    _addConfidenceHighlight(
      session,
      highlights,
    );

    _addCollectorHighlights(
      session.collectorStatus,
      highlights,
    );

    _addVariantHighlights(
      session.variantResolution,
      highlights,
    );

    _addMarketHighlights(
      session.marketSnapshot,
      highlights,
    );

    _addSessionHighlights(
      session,
      highlights,
    );

    return AtlasScanBriefing(
      priority: _priorityFor(session),
      title: _titleFor(session),
      summary: _summaryFor(session),
      highlights: List<String>.unmodifiable(
        highlights,
      ),
      recommendation: _recommendationFor(session),
    );
  }

  static AtlasScanBriefingPriority _priorityFor(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    if (session.resolutionStatus ==
        IdentityResolutionStatus.failed ||
        session.status == ScanSessionStatus.failed) {
      return AtlasScanBriefingPriority.critical;
    }

    if (session.requiresUserAction ||
        session.resolutionStatus ==
            IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous ||
        session.resolutionStatus ==
            IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved) {
      return AtlasScanBriefingPriority.warning;
    }

    if (session.hasResolvedProduct) {
      return AtlasScanBriefingPriority.success;
    }

    return AtlasScanBriefingPriority.information;
  }

  static String _titleFor(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    switch (session.resolutionStatus) {
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.failed:
        return 'Atlas Could Not Resolve This Product';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous:
        return 'Product Review Required';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved:
        return 'Product Identity Unresolved';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.pendingSync:
        return 'Product Resolved — Sync Pending';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedLocal:
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedRemote:
        return 'Atlas Scan Briefing';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolving:
        return 'Atlas Is Resolving This Product';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted:
        return 'Scan Evidence Collected';
    }
  }

  static String _summaryFor(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    switch (session.resolutionStatus) {
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.failed:
        return 'Atlas could not confidently identify this collectible from the available scan evidence.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous:
        return 'Atlas found more than one possible product identity. The collectible should be reviewed before it is added to the Vault.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved:
        return 'The scan was processed, but Atlas could not connect the available evidence to a confirmed product identity.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.pendingSync:
        return 'Atlas resolved this collectible locally. Additional connected intelligence will be synchronized when available.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedLocal:
        return 'Atlas successfully identified this collectible using locally available intelligence.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedRemote:
        return 'Atlas successfully identified this collectible using connected catalog intelligence.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolving:
        return 'Atlas is still analyzing the available product evidence.';

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted:
        return 'Atlas collected the barcode evidence, but identity resolution has not started.';
    }
  }

  static String _recommendationFor(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    if (session.resolutionStatus ==
        IdentityResolutionStatus.failed ||
        session.status == ScanSessionStatus.failed) {
      return 'Try scanning the product barcode again or search the Atlas catalog manually.';
    }

    if (session.requiresVariantConfirmation) {
      return 'Confirm the sticker, finish, exclusive, packaging, and other variant traits before adding this collectible.';
    }

    if (session.resolutionStatus ==
        IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous) {
      return 'Review each possible catalog match and select the exact physical release.';
    }

    if (session.resolutionStatus ==
        IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved) {
      return 'Search the broader catalog or enter the product manually.';
    }

    final CollectorItemStatus? collectorStatus =
        session.collectorStatus;

    if (collectorStatus?.hasPossibleDuplicates ?? false) {
      return 'Review the possible duplicate records before adding another copy.';
    }

    if (collectorStatus?.isOwned ?? false) {
      return 'This product is already represented in your Vault. Add it only if this is another copy or a distinct variant.';
    }

    if (collectorStatus?.isWishlisted ?? false) {
      return 'This product is on your wishlist and can be added to your owned collection after confirming the release details.';
    }

    if (session.hasResolvedProduct) {
      return 'The product identity is resolved. Review the details and add it to your Vault when ready.';
    }

    return 'Review the scan evidence before continuing.';
  }

  static void _addConfidenceHighlight(
      ScanSession session,
      List<String> highlights,
      ) {
    final int? confidencePercent =
    _confidencePercent(session.confidence);

    if (confidencePercent == null) {
      return;
    }

    highlights.add(
      'Atlas confidence: $confidencePercent%',
    );
  }

  static void _addCollectorHighlights(
      CollectorItemStatus? status,
      List<String> highlights,
      ) {
    if (status == null) {
      return;
    }

    switch (status.relationship) {
      case CollectorItemRelationship.notInVault:
        highlights.add(
          'This release is not currently in your Vault.',
        );

      case CollectorItemRelationship.owned:
        highlights.add(
          _ownedQuantityMessage(status.ownedQuantity),
        );

      case CollectorItemRelationship.wishlisted:
        highlights.add(
          'This release is currently on your wishlist.',
        );

      case CollectorItemRelationship.ownedAndWishlisted:
        highlights
          ..add(
            _ownedQuantityMessage(status.ownedQuantity),
          )
          ..add(
            'This release is also on your wishlist.',
          );

      case CollectorItemRelationship.unknown:
        break;
    }

    if (status.collectionNames.isNotEmpty) {
      highlights.add(
        'Vault location: ${status.collectionNames.join(', ')}',
      );
    }

    if (status.hasPossibleDuplicates) {
      final int count = status.possibleDuplicateCount;

      highlights.add(
        '$count possible duplicate ${count == 1 ? 'record' : 'records'} found.',
      );
    }
  }

  static String _ownedQuantityMessage(
      int quantity,
      ) {
    if (quantity <= 1) {
      return 'You already own this release.';
    }

    return 'You already own $quantity copies of this release.';
  }

  static void _addVariantHighlights(
      ScanVariantResolution? resolution,
      List<String> highlights,
      ) {
    if (resolution == null) {
      return;
    }

    switch (resolution.status) {
      case ScanVariantStatus.exactMatch:
        highlights.add(
          'The exact physical variant was identified.',
        );

      case ScanVariantStatus.likelyMatch:
        highlights.add(
          'Atlas found a likely variant match.',
        );

      case ScanVariantStatus.multipleCandidates:
        final int count =
            resolution.candidateProductIds.length;

        highlights.add(
          count > 1
              ? '$count possible product variants were found.'
              : 'More than one possible variant may apply.',
        );

      case ScanVariantStatus.visualConfirmationRequired:
        highlights.add(
          'Visual variant confirmation is required.',
        );

      case ScanVariantStatus.unknown:
        break;
    }

    if (resolution.confirmedTraits.isNotEmpty) {
      highlights.add(
        'Confirmed traits: ${resolution.confirmedTraits.join(', ')}',
      );
    }

    if (resolution.unconfirmedTraits.isNotEmpty) {
      highlights.add(
        'Review traits: ${resolution.unconfirmedTraits.join(', ')}',
      );
    }

    final int? confidencePercent =
    _confidencePercent(resolution.confidence);

    if (confidencePercent != null) {
      highlights.add(
        'Variant confidence: $confidencePercent%',
      );
    }
  }

  static void _addMarketHighlights(
      MarketValueSnapshot? snapshot,
      List<String> highlights,
      ) {
    if (snapshot == null ||
        !snapshot.hasMarketEvidence) {
      return;
    }

    if (snapshot.hasEstimatedValue) {
      highlights.add(
        'Estimated value: ${_formatMoney(
          snapshot.estimatedValue!,
          snapshot.currencyCode,
        )}',
      );
    }

    if (snapshot.hasRecentRange) {
      highlights.add(
        'Recent sale range: '
            '${_formatMoney(
          snapshot.recentSaleLow!,
          snapshot.currencyCode,
        )}–'
            '${_formatMoney(
          snapshot.recentSaleHigh!,
          snapshot.currencyCode,
        )}',
      );
    } else if (snapshot.hasLastSale) {
      highlights.add(
        'Last recorded sale: ${_formatMoney(
          snapshot.lastSalePrice!,
          snapshot.currencyCode,
        )}',
      );
    }

    if (snapshot.hasTrend) {
      final double percentage =
      snapshot.trendPercentage!.abs();

      switch (snapshot.trendDirection) {
        case MarketTrendDirection.rising:
          highlights.add(
            'Market trend: rising ${percentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
          );

        case MarketTrendDirection.falling:
          highlights.add(
            'Market trend: falling ${percentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
          );

        case MarketTrendDirection.stable:
          highlights.add(
            'Market trend: stable',
          );

        case MarketTrendDirection.unknown:
          highlights.add(
            'Recent market movement: ${percentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
          );
      }
    }

    if (snapshot.isStale) {
      highlights.add(
        'Market information may be outdated.',
      );
    }
  }

  static void _addSessionHighlights(
      ScanSession session,
      List<String> highlights,
      ) {
    if (session.isOffline) {
      highlights.add(
        'This scan was processed offline.',
      );
    }

    if (session.requiresSync) {
      highlights.add(
        'Additional intelligence is waiting to sync.',
      );
    }

    if (session.contributionCount > 0) {
      final int count = session.contributionCount;

      highlights.add(
        '$count intelligence ${count == 1 ? 'contribution' : 'contributions'} evaluated.',
      );
    }

    if (session.warnings.isNotEmpty) {
      final int count = session.warnings.length;

      highlights.add(
        '$count scan ${count == 1 ? 'warning requires' : 'warnings require'} review.',
      );
    }
  }

  static int? _confidencePercent(
      double confidence,
      ) {
    if (!confidence.isFinite || confidence <= 0) {
      return null;
    }

    final double normalized =
    confidence <= 1 ? confidence * 100 : confidence;

    return normalized.round().clamp(0, 100);
  }

  static String _formatMoney(
      double value,
      String currencyCode,
      ) {
    final String normalizedCurrency =
    currencyCode.trim().toUpperCase();

    final String symbol;

    switch (normalizedCurrency) {
      case 'USD':
        symbol = r'$';

      case 'CAD':
        symbol = r'CA$';

      case 'GBP':
        symbol = '£';

      case 'EUR':
        symbol = '€';

      default:
        symbol = normalizedCurrency.isEmpty
            ? ''
            : '$normalizedCurrency ';
    }

    return '$symbol${value.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }
}

import '../../scan/models/scan_session.dart';
import '../../scan/workflow/identity_resolution_status.dart';
import '../models/atlas_scan_intelligence.dart';
import 'atlas_engine.dart';

class AtlasScanIntelligenceEngine
    extends AtlasEngine<ScanSession, AtlasScanIntelligence> {
  const AtlasScanIntelligenceEngine();

  @override
  String get id => 'atlas.scan-intelligence';

  @override
  String get name => 'Atlas Scan Intelligence Engine';

  @override
  String get description {
    return 'Produces prioritized collector-facing intelligence from a '
        'projected Atlas scan session.';
  }

  @override
  String get version => '1.1.0';

  @override
  List<String> validateInput(
      ScanSession input,
      ) {
    final List<String> issues = <String>[];

    if (input.sessionId.trim().isEmpty) {
      issues.add(
        'The scan session must have a session ID.',
      );
    }

    if (!input.hasDetectedCodes) {
      issues.add(
        'The scan session does not contain detected code evidence.',
      );
    }

    return List<String>.unmodifiable(
      issues,
    );

  }
  List<AtlasScanInsight> _deduplicateInsights(
      List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
      ) {
    final Map<String, AtlasScanInsight> byId =
    <String, AtlasScanInsight>{};

    for (final AtlasScanInsight insight in insights) {
      final AtlasScanInsight? existing =
      byId[insight.id];

      if (existing == null ||
          insight.effectivePriorityWeight <
              existing.effectivePriorityWeight) {
        byId[insight.id] = insight;
      }
    }

    return byId.values.toList(
      growable: false,
    );
  }

  @override
  AtlasScanIntelligence process(
      ScanSession input,
      ) {
    final List<AtlasScanInsight> insights =
    <AtlasScanInsight>[];

    _addIdentityInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addConfidenceInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addVariantInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addCollectorInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addMarketInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addSyncInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addWarningInsights(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    final List<AtlasScanInsight> finalInsights =
    _deduplicateInsights(
      insights,
    );

    finalInsights.sort(
          (
          AtlasScanInsight first,
          AtlasScanInsight second,
          ) {
        final int priorityComparison =
        first.effectivePriorityWeight.compareTo(
          second.effectivePriorityWeight,
        );

        if (priorityComparison != 0) {
          return priorityComparison;
        }

        return first.id.compareTo(
          second.id,
        );
      },
    );

    return AtlasScanIntelligence(
      sessionId: input.sessionId,
      generatedAt: DateTime.now(),
      insights: finalInsights,
    );
  }

  void _addIdentityInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    switch (session.resolutionStatus) {
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedLocal:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-resolved-local',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '✅',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible using local intelligence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedRemote:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-resolved-remote',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '🌐',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible using connected catalog intelligence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: true,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.pendingSync:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-sync-pending',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '✅',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible locally. Additional connected intelligence is pending.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-ambiguous',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '⚠️',
            title: 'Identity Review Required',
            message:
            'Atlas found more than one possible product identity.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
            priorityWeight: 10,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Review the product, barcode, sticker, and variant details before continuing.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-unresolved',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '🔎',
            title: 'Product Not Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas could not determine a confirmed product identity from the available evidence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
            priorityWeight: 10,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Try scanning the barcode again or search the Atlas catalog manually.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.failed:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-failed',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '🚨',
            title: 'Resolution Failed',
            message:
            'Atlas could not complete product identity resolution.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.critical,
            priorityWeight: 5,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.system,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Review the scan evidence and attempt a new scan.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted:
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolving:
        break;
    }
  }

  void _addConfidenceInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    final int? confidencePercent = _confidencePercent(
      session.confidence,
    );

    if (confidencePercent == null) {
      return;
    }

    final AtlasScanInsightPriority priority;
    final int priorityWeight;
    final String message;

    if (confidencePercent >= 90) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.low;
      priorityWeight = 60;
      message =
      'Atlas has very high confidence in this product identification.';
    } else if (confidencePercent >= 75) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal;
      priorityWeight = 55;
      message =
      'Atlas has strong confidence in this product identification.';
    } else if (confidencePercent >= 60) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal;
      priorityWeight = 40;
      message =
      'Atlas has moderate confidence in this product identification.';
    } else {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.high;
      priorityWeight = 20;
      message =
      'Atlas has limited confidence in this product identification.';
    }

    insights.add(
      AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'scan-confidence',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
        emoji: '🎯',
        title: 'Identification Confidence',
        message: message,
        priority: priority,
        priorityWeight: priorityWeight,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.scan,
        requiresOnline: false,
        details: <String>[
          '$confidencePercent% confidence',
          '${session.contributionCount} intelligence '
              '${session.contributionCount == 1 ? 'contribution' : 'contributions'}',
        ],
        recommendation: confidencePercent < 60
            ? 'Confirm the product number, sticker, and variant before adding it to your Vault.'
            : null,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addVariantInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasVariantResolution) {
      return;
    }

    if (session.requiresVariantConfirmation) {
      insights.add(
        const AtlasScanInsight(
          id: 'variant-confirmation-required',
          type: AtlasScanInsightType.variant,
          emoji: '🧩',
          title: 'Variant Confirmation Required',
          message:
          'Atlas identified possible variant information that still needs collector confirmation.',
          priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
          priorityWeight: 15,
          source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
          requiresOnline: false,
          recommendation:
          'Compare the sticker, finish, retailer exclusive, event, and packaging details.',
        ),
      );

      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'variant-reviewed',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.variant,
        emoji: '🏷️',
        title: 'Variant Evidence Reviewed',
        message:
        'Atlas processed the available variant evidence for this collectible.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.low,
        priorityWeight: 80,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
        requiresOnline: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addCollectorInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasCollectorStatus) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'collector-status-available',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.collection,
        emoji: '📦',
        title: 'Vault Comparison Available',
        message:
        'Atlas compared this collectible with the collector’s current Vault.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 50,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.collection,
        requiresOnline: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addMarketInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasMarketData) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'market-evidence-available',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.market,
        emoji: '💰',
        title: 'Market Evidence Available',
        message:
        'Atlas found market evidence connected to this collectible.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 40,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.market,
        requiresOnline: true,
        recommendation:
        'Review the market snapshot before recording or changing the item value.',
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addSyncInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.requiresSync) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'sync-required',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.recommendation,
        emoji: '☁️',
        title: 'Additional Intelligence Pending',
        message: session.isOffline
            ? 'Atlas is currently offline and cannot complete connected intelligence checks.'
            : 'Atlas has additional intelligence that still needs to synchronize.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 70,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.system,
        requiresOnline: true,
        recommendation:
        'Keep the scan session available until synchronization completes.',
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addWarningInsights({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    for (
    int index = 0;
    index < session.warnings.length;
    index++
    ) {
      final String warning = session.warnings[index].trim();

      if (warning.isEmpty) {
        continue;
      }

      insights.add(
        AtlasScanInsight(
          id: 'session-warning-$index',
          type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
          emoji: '🚨',
          title: 'Scan Warning',
          message: warning,
          priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
          priorityWeight: 5,
          source: AtlasScanInsightSource.scan,
          requiresOnline: false,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  List<String> _identityDetails(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    final List<String> details = <String>[];

    final String? productId =
    session.atlasProductId?.value.trim();

    if (productId != null && productId.isNotEmpty) {
      details.add(
        'Atlas Product ID: $productId',
      );
    }

    final String? productName =
    session.catalogProduct?.displayName.trim();

    if (productName != null && productName.isNotEmpty) {
      details.add(
        productName,
      );
    }

    return List<String>.unmodifiable(
      details,
    );
  }

  int? _confidencePercent(
      double confidence,
      ) {
    if (!confidence.isFinite || confidence <= 0) {
      return null;
    }

    final double normalized =
    confidence <= 1
        ? confidence * 100
        : confidence;

    return normalized.round().clamp(
      0,
      100,
    );
  }
}
